pṛ0 1a) śrīgaṇeśāyanamaḥ caṃdrārdhacūḍa(caṃdrasyaarddhacandrārddhaca * * * * * yasya * * * * ? rddhacūḍaḥtasyacaraṇābjayugaṃ||āgama?yuktibhirgūḍhaṃāgamayukti?gūḍhāgama?yuktiguḍha*?kramyitiyathāgamaśaktigūḍhaṃ|| ) caraṇābjayugaṃpraṇamyavāgīśvarīṃgaṇapatiṃkramaśoguruṃśca|leśādyathāmatiyathāgamayuktigū
ḍhaṃśrīmanmṛgeṃdraparamārthamahaṃvyanajmi 1 kecitsvabodhavibhavaprathanāyatāvadgarjaṃtyaho	vratanayatprakṛtopayogi|a
nyepunaḥpaṭudhiyonavivecayaṃtispaṣṭārthametadititattadupekṣamāṇāḥ 2 saṃkṣepataḥprakṛtavastuparisphuṭaṃyevyācakṣatepadapadā
rthasamanvayeta| prāyaḥkuśāgramatibhistihādriyaṃtevyākhyākṛtoviphala(kuśāgramatibhiḥ capunaḥ viphalavistarabhīrubhistevyākhyākṛtādriyaṃte)vistarabhīrubhiśca 3 sākṣātchrīkaṃṭhanāthādi
vasukṛtijanānugrahāyāvatīrṇātchrutvāśrīrāmakaṃṭhātchivamatakamalonmīlanaprauḍhabhāsvān|śrīvidyākaṃṭhabhaṭṭastadi
damupadiśannādideśaikadā(ekadāśrīvidyākaṃṭhabhaṭṭaḥmāṃtatmṛgeṃdraṃupadiśan pāṭhayan idaṃādideśidaṃkiṃtevatsavaṃatraspaṣṭārthāṃlaghvīṃsarvopayogyāṃvivṛtiṃviracaya)māṃspaṣṭārthāmatralaghvīviracayavivṛtiṃvatsasarvopayogyāṃ 4 uparyuparyevakṛtānikaiścinmatāṃ
tarāṇāṃhinivaṃdhanāni|nodbhinnamudraṃtvidamagha(adyayāvatadyārabhyabhyanudbhinnāmudrāyasyatatudbhinnamudraṃnavyākhyātamityarthaḥ)yāvattasmādgururmāmidamanvaśātsaḥ 5 tenāhamasyāṃvivṛtopravṛttonollaṃgha
nīyāhiguroranujñā|yadṛchayākotigabhīraśavdaṃmṛgeṃdramākṣeptamidaṃvyavasyet 6 kimapiyadihanoktaṃyaccakiṃcidduruktaṃkathi

pṛ0 1ba) tamapimayāyatprasphuṭaṃnāvabhāti|dinakṛtivadoṣāpahnavaika(doṣāṇāṃapahnavaḥdoṣopahnavaḥ ekaṃmukhyaṃpravṛtāekapravṛttāḥdoṣāpahnaveekapravṛttāḥ)pravṛttāstadapi(doṣāpahnavaikapravṛttāḥ)vivudhamukhyāḥspaṣṭayaṃtaḥ(spaṣṭīkurvaṃtaḥ)kṣamaṃtāṃ 7 ataḥ
paraṃpravarttaṃtāṃvṛttayopyuttarottarāḥ|prākjvālitātpradīpādvikasvarādīpasaṃtatiḥ 8 athānādimalāpetityāditattaṃtraṃ
yatpunaḥparameśaṃnamaskṛtyetyādyupodghātprakaraṇaṃ|tatsaṃvaṃdhābhidheyaprayojanataṃtrāvatārakramanidarśanārthaṃ|purākhalu
tatrabhavānbharadvājaśiṣyaḥsamadhigatasakalavedaśāstrārthasatatvomunirhārītanāmāśivaśaktyunmukhīkṛtace
tobhiḥsamāsāditadīkṣaiḥjñānakriyācaryāyogajijñāsubhiḥnaimiśāraṇyavāsibhistapodhanaiḥkṛtapraṇāmāṃja
libhirūrdhasrotaḥprasṛtaṃśrīmatkāmikabhedaṃmṛgeṃdrottarasaṃjñakaṃpṛṣṭaḥsatānśiṣyaguṇopapannāndṛṣṭvāparameśvarādi
praṇāmapūrvakamidamāhetihārītaśiṣyastatpraśiṣyovāsvagurūktamanukurvansvaśiṣyānidamavadat|parameśaṃnama
skṛtyabharadvājamṛṣiṃtataḥ|harādiṃdrakramāyātaṃjñānaṃśṛṇutasuvratāḥ 1 hesuvratāḥparameśvarabhaktiprakarṣaparipoṣitaśo

pṛ0 2a) bhātiśayatvātsuṣṭuśobhanaṃvrataṃśāstrīyaniyamānuṣṭhānaṃyeṣāṃtevaśrotāraḥsaṃvodhitāḥ|yadvakṣyatyupasaṃhāre|bhavatsuśivaśī
tāṃśujyotsnāpāṃḍuṣvidaṃmayā|tadichayaivoktamiti|(śivechaivoktaṃ)harādiṃdrakramāyātaṃ|jñānaṃśruṇutākarṇayatetisavaṃdhaḥ|haratipaśu
bhyaḥpāśānpuṃsopyūrdhvaṃ(puṃstvoparivarttamānaḥ)tatastuhariti yadyapiyogikīyaṃsaṃjñā anaṃteśādiṣvapisāmānyā|tathāpiiṃdraśavdasaṃnidheri
homāpatāvevaharaśavdojñeyaḥ|yathāhuḥ|arthaḥprakaraṇaṃliṃgaṃśavdasyānyasyasaṃnidhiḥ|proktāḥsāmānyaśavdānāṃviśeṣasthitihetaviti|kutetaditicet|yasmādaiṃdreasminkāmikabhedebhagavatumāpatervaktṛtveneṃdrasyacaśrotṛtvenaiva
saṃvaṃdhaḥpratītaḥ|tathāhi|purastādihaivamunīnāmiṃdrovakṣyati|śivodgīrṇamidaṃ jñānaṃmaṃtramaṃtreśvareśvaraiḥ|(vidyātatvādhikāriṇaḥsaptakoṭayomaṃtrāstadātmakādevāḥ|| maṃtreśvarāḥanaṃtādayaḥ|īśvaratatvādhikārī * * * * * * * * * * * *?)kāmada
tvātkāmiketipragītaṃvahuvistaraṃ|tebhyo'vagatyadṛgjyotirjvālālīḍhasmaradrumaḥ|ihāvumāpatirmahyamiti|tasmā
ddharātśrīkaṃṭhanāthādiṃdrādikrameṇāyātamitiavatīrṇaṃ|kiṃtat|jñāyaṃte'nenavidyācaryākriyāyogāitijñānaṃ

pṛ0 2ba) śāstraṃ|kiṃkṛtvāśṛṇuta|parameśvaraṃnamaskṛtya|sṛṣṭisthitisaṃkṣobhaṇādibhiścidacillakṣaṇaṃviśvamīṣṭeitīśaḥ|
īdṛktvaṃcāṃnaṃteśādīnāmapyastītiparamapadenaviśeṣaṇaṃ|paramaścāsāvanyeṣāṃtadanugrahataḥśivatvābhivyakteḥīśaśca
svātaṃtryeṇasthittyādikaraṇāt parameśastanamaskṛtya|kāyavāṅmanobhistasminprahvībhūya|tatityanaṃtaraṃtaṃtrāva
tārakaṃbharadvājamṛṣimiti|(ṛchatijānātiitiṛṣiḥ)kragatāvitidhātvarthataḥsarveṣāṃcagatyarthānāṃjñānārthatvādabagataparamārthatayāṛṣistaṃ
namaskṛtyaśrṛṇutetiśrotṝṇāṃnamaskāropadeśaḥ|anamaskṛtaparameśvarāṇāṃapraṇatagurūṇāṃcanirvighnasaṃhi
tādhigamanāsaṃbhavāt|yaścaśrotṛjanamevaṃśikṣayati so'rthātkṛtatathāvidhavidhiḥpravṛttitigamyate|tena
yathāparameśvaraṃguruṃcanatvā'hamupadeṣṭuṃpravṛttastayāpūpamapiśrotuṃpravṛrttadhvamitivyavasthitaṃ|yathācaitadiṃdrasyomāpa
tināupadiṣṭaṃyattadgraṃthaparisamāptauyadyapigraṃtheevāstitathāpivyākhyānopakramesaṃvaṃdhāderavaśyābhidheyatvātkiṃciducyate| 

pṛ0 3a) kadācidatiruciraratnaprakaravinicitakanakamayakamanīyamahītalamanoharasaptamajaladhitaṭaviharaṇahe(caturaṃ)bākinaṃnīlo
palatviṣaṃcaturbhujaṃdvikaṃdharādharaṃmukhadvayenavedādhyayanasurāpānasaṃkarakāriṇaṃasuranivahaparivṛtaṃdurmadaṃmahāsuraṃtri
daśapatirapaśyat|athatathāvidhadurācāradarśanajanitakopākulitamunigaṇasahitaḥśatakraturakṣamamāṇolavdha
varatvātprasabhamastrairavadhyatāmasyavudhvāphenenāṃtarnihitavajreṇāsurasyaśirodvayaṃcicheda|tadvyāpādanataścavrahmaha
tyājanitamadyamāṃśakamānastatpraśāṃtayebhagavaṃtamacyutaṃnāmnāṃsahasreṇṛgyajuḥsāmabhiścāstauṣīt|prasaṃnnenacahari
ṇānavoditabhāsvatbhāsvaraṃsarvabhūtābhibhāvukaṃnārasiṃhaṃkavacaṃprayachatāproktaṃ|yathaitatsannaddhadehastaṃuttarakuruṣubhaga
vaṃtaṃpinākinamārādhayavarṣasahasrāṃtecataṃdṛṣṭvā'bhimatamāsādayiṣyasīttyuktāṃtarhitemurārausarvaṃtadiṃdreṇakṛtaṃ|a
thapratyakṣībhūtebhagavatipinākininikhiladuḥkhāṃtaścāsyasaṃjātaḥśrīmatkāmikākhyaṃcāsmaiparameśvarupadideśa|ya

pṛ0 3ba) taśceṃdrasyanṛsiṃharūpiṇaḥsamupadiṣṭamidamumāpatinā|tatomṛgeṃdrasyaśrotṛtvānmṛgeṃdrasaṃjñayāprathitaṃ|saṃvaṃdhastvatraṣaṭ
prakāraḥparādiḥ|tathāhi|sargādauparameśvaraḥūrdhvaprākdakṣiṇottarapaścimasrotaḥpaṃcakabhedabhinnaṃjñānaṃsvechānugṛhīta
vidyeśvarāṣṭakapravodhanānaṃtaraṃ(pralayeyesuptāḥsthitānaṃtādayasteṣāṃvodhānaṃtaraṃprathamamevaabhivyaktaṃyattadeva maṃtreśvarādibhyavakṣamāṇasadṛśaṃādideśetyarthaḥ)tadabhivyaktaṃmaṃtreśvarādibhyovakṣyamāṇavadādideśa|tathācoktaṃśrīmatkiraṇe|sṛṣṭyanaṃta
rameveśaḥśivānsṛṣṭvādṛśātmajān|jñānamekaṃvibhajyāśuteṣāṃtatsaṃkhyayāvadat|kāmikaṃpraṇavākhyasyaśivākhya
syatuyogajamityādi|atracātmajānitiātmanaivaniradhikaraṇenānugraheṇānugṛhītānityāśayaḥ|tebhyaḥumāpatiḥ
prāpa|tasmāccaśakraḥ|tatopibharadvājaḥ|tatsakāśāttuhārītaḥ|sapunaḥsvaśiṣyebhyaḥprāhetipurastātsarvaṃspaṣṭīkari
ṣyāmaḥ|abhidheyāścehajñānakriyāyogacaryāḥ|prayojanaṃtvatratatvajñaptiḥ|tatprayojanamapibhuktimuktī|parameśvarasadbhā
vaprasādhakapramāṇopanyāsastadvādhakanirākaraṇaṃca yadyapitatpraṇītāgamaprāmāṇyasādhanāyaprathamamevopayujyateta

pṛ0 4a) thāpiśāstrakāreṇaivāgretadvivecitaṃ|atastatraivavakṣyāmaḥ|kevalametāvadevehapratijānīmahe|yaduktaṃāyurvedācca(amukoṣadhenāmukarogogacchatiitiāyurvedenīśvarapraṇītena|gaṇitenasūryādigrahaṇajñānaṃ||maṃtravādātmaṃtrābhijaptapānīyāditāḍanādinābhūtādināśaḥ||)gaṇitānmaṃ
tranādāccasasvarāt(svarātgāṃdharvavidyayāmeghadīpādyutpattiḥ|)rasopaniṣadāṭopādāptaḥ(rasopaniṣadātdhāturvādātsuvarṇādyutpattiritiāyurvedādināīśvaraḥsiddhaḥ)saparigṛhyatiti|taccāptatvaṃ(avidyāvṛtatvāt)sāṃjanatvādasarvārthadṛśāṃdarśanāṃtarapraṇetṛṇāṃsva
svaviṣayameva|nityanirmalaniratiśayasarvārthajñānakriyāśaktestusarvadāsarvānugrahapravṛttasyaparameśvaratvādevasarvādhiṣṭhātuḥ
sarvaviṣayaṃ|evaṃvividhaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭasyavakturvipralaṃbhakatvāsaṃbhavānnāprāmāṇyaṃ|etaccayathāvasaraṃvakṣyāmaḥ|tadeva
mīdṛkrūpobhagavānāgamasyakarttā|āgamastutaṃjñāpayati|ataścadvayorapivisadṛśakriyatvānnātretaretarāśrayado
ṣaḥ(vijātīyakriyātvāt)syāt|yathākaścidevaṃvakti|devadattohamāyātiti|tadvākyaśravaṇāccatadanyemanyaṃte|hatadevadattoyamāpātiti 
|nacātradevadattasyatadvākyasyacetaretarāśrayatvaṃyuktaṃ|tadukteṣu(īśvarokteṣu)cadṛṣṭārtheṣuviṣabhūtarasavādādiṣu(garuḍādimaṃtrāviṣabhūtādināśakāḥ)tadāgamasya(īśvarāgamasya)phalavatvamu
palabhyādṛṣṭārthasyāpitachāsanasyāvitathatvamanumīyateityalamanenaprakṛtamanusarāmaḥ|tadevaṃhārītamuniḥsvaśiṣyā

pṛ0 4ba) ṇāṃjñānopadeśaṃpratijñāyeṃdrakramāyātatvaṃdarśayituṃāha|nārāyaṇāśramepuṇyebharadvājāvayodvijāḥ|tepuḥśivaṃpratiṣṭhāpyata
dekāhitamānasāḥ|vradaryāśramanāmniviṣṇorāśrametadāśramatvādevapāvanebharadvājaprabhṛtayomunayastepuritisaṃvaṃdhaḥ|bharadvājā
dīnāmṛṣīṇāmadvijatvaprasiddhyasaṃbhavātdvijāitiviśeṣaṇaṃvāgīśvarīgarbhasaṃyojanasaṃjananādinākṛtadīkṣātvenotka
rṣavatvaṃnapunarupanītatvamātraṃsmārttavatdvijaśavdeneṣṭaṃ|adīkṣitānāṃtaṃtrādiśravaṇānadhikārāt|pratyutapratyavāyaśruteḥ 
|śivaṃpratiṣṭhāpyeti|lohavāṇaliṃgādāvādhāresāmānyamaṃtrādinā(prathamaṃtusāmānyamaṃtravidhināśivasthāpanaṃkṛtaṃtaduttaraṃviśeṣapratiṣṭhājijñāsayāviśeṣaprakṣmāpikariṣyaṃti)parameśvarapratiṣṭhāpanaṃparikalpyetyāśayaḥ|anya
thāyathāvadviditatattadvidhānānāṃpurastātpratiṣṭhādiviṣayasyapraśnasyānupapatteḥ|tasminnevaikasminnāhitamekāgrīkṛ
taṃmānasaṃyaistetathāvidhāḥsaṃtaḥ|teṣuḥśivārādhanalakṣaṇaṃtapaścakrurityarthaḥ|athatānbhāvitānmatvākadācittridaśā
dhipaḥtadāśramapadaṃbhejesvayaṃtāpasaveṣabhṛtanaṃtaraṃcatānbhāvitāttaṃtraśraddhālūnjñātvākasmiṃścitkālemunirūpadhārīśa

pṛ0 5a) kraḥtadīyamāśramaṃsiṣeve|tataśca|sataiḥsaṃpūjitaḥpṛṣṭvātāṃścasarvānanāmayaṃprovāca|codanādharmaḥkimarthaṃnānuvartyate|siṃdrastai
rbharadvājādibhirāśramasamucitenātithisatkāreṇābhyarcitestānbharadvājādīnpratyekaṃkuśalaṃpṛṣṭvāvravīt|kiṃtadityata
āha|codanādharmaḥkimarthaṃnānuvarttyatiti|codanetikriyāyāḥpravarttakaṃ(vidhi)vacanamāhuḥ|tadāmnātodharmaḥkimitinānu
ṣṭhīyate|codanaiva(viśeṣyasaṃbaddhevakāraḥanyayogaṃvyavachinatti|yathāpārthevadhanurddharaḥpārthivasadṛśo'nyodhanurddharonāstītyarthaḥ||evaṃviśeṣaṇasaṃbaddhevakāraayogaṃvyavachinatti||yathā||śaṃkhaḥpāṃḍureva||tadvadatrāpicodanaivahipramāṇaṃpramāṇamevacodanetikrameṇevakāroviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇasaṃbaddhaanyayogāyogauvyavachinatti||)hidharmepramāṇaṃpramāṇamevacodanetyevamanyayogāyogavyavachedanenatataḥpravarttamānāmaihikasyāmu
trikasyacaphalasyāvisaṃvādāt|(avaśyabhāvitvāt)tathācoktaṃśrutismṛtyuditaṃdharmamanutiṣṭhahnimānavaḥ|ihakīrttimavāpnotipretyacānutta
māṃgatimiti|tadviparītaṃtutrayīvāhyaṃliṃgārādhanādicayattattrayībāhyatvādevaphaluprāyaṃ|yadāhabhaṭṭaḥ|tathātikrāṃtave
doktamaryādāvyavahāriṇāṃsaṃvādiṣvapivākyeṣu(nāstikādipraṇītāsaṃdigdhārtheṣvapicaityavaṃdanādivākyeṣu)neṣyatemānahetutā|manurapi|yāvedavāhyāsmṛtayoyāścakāścitkudṛṣṭayaḥ(cārvākādipraṇītagraṃthāḥ) 
|sarvāstāniṣphalāḥpretyatamobhūtāhitāḥsmṛtāḥ|ityevaṃjaiminimatānusāriṇaḥ|tasyavacaḥśrutvā|tūcurnanvayaṃdharma

pṛ0 5ba) ścodanāvihitomune|devatārādhanopāyastapasābhīṣṭasiddhaye|tebharadvājādayaḥ|iṃdraṃtāpasarūpatvāt muniśavdenāmaṃ
tryananvitiprativacanamavodhan|yoyamasmābhirabhihitorudrākhyadevatāprasādanopāyalakṣaṇodharmaḥtapasāsamīhitasi
dhyarthaṃāsevyatesacodanayaivābhi hitovyavasthāpitaḥ|tathāhi|codanānāmaliṃṅloṭtavyadādiśavdavyavasthāpitavi
dhiniṣedharūpayajanādikriyāpravarttakavacanamabhidhīyate|yathājyotiṣṭhomenayajetasvargakāmenakabhaṃjaṃbhakṣayedityādi|taccamukhyatayā
śrautaṃdharmarūpaṃtanmūlatvāccasmārttamapi|tathācoktaṃ|tasmātśrutismṛtīevapramāṇaṃdharmagocariti|tathāvedo' khi
lodharmamūlaṃsmṛtiśīle(vedavipraṃakhilopivedaḥsmṛtirmanvādiḥśīlaṃācaraṇaṃcadharmamūlaṃbhavati)catadvidāṃ|ititadāstāṃtāvadevaṃvidhāśrutyādisadāgamārthāvirodhinyaḥpaurāṇikyaḥsaṃhi
tāḥ|anyāścakāścanaśrutayoyāsāmidaṃtātparyaṃ|yathāmahābhāratādau|yebhaktāvaradaṃdevaṃśivaṃrudramumāpatiṃ(umāpatiṃarcayaṃtītiśeṣaḥ)ihalokesu
khaṃprāpyateyāṃtiparamāṃgatimiti|sarvarūpaṃbhavaṃjñātvāliṃgeyorcayatiprabhuṃ|tasminnabhyadhikāprītiṃkarotivṛṣabhadhvajaḥ 

pṛ0 6a) ititathāādityāvasavorudrāmunayaścamahaujasaḥ|vidhivalliṃgamārādhyapadamiṣṭatamaṃgatāiti|tathāmṛtbhasmagośakṛ
tpiṣṭaguḍakhaṃḍādiliṃgakaṃ|saṃpūjayanpratiṣṭhāpyaśraddhāvānadhigacchatīti|taṃtrāprāmāṇyāmabhyupagamyāpivrūmaḥ|yadabhiprāyeṇedamabhi

pṛ0 6ba) mātramevāsti|tatratatrarudrevadevatāvācyarūpatayāśrūyate|yamuddiśyeṣaterudrabhāgaḥsahasvasrāṃvikapātaṃjuṣasvasvāhe

pṛ0 7ba) dādivat|śavdavyatiriktādityadidevatāvidyatekiṃvigrahavatyavigrahobhayarūpā'nubhavarūpāvānubhayarūpatve'vastutvādyatkiṃcitta

pṛ0 9ba) kārapuṣkariṇyādivipakṣavyāvṛttaścāyaṃ hetuḥyatrakilātmādaukāryatvaṃnāstitatrasāvayavatvamapināstīti|nacāsya(nacityasyitipadenasahaanvayaḥ)he

pṛ0 10a) dṛśyāvalaṃvanapūrvavyāptidarśanāhitasaṃskārācatadanuguṇasādhya(yādṛśaṃsādhyaṃyādṛśaṃsādhakaṃtādṛśamevānumitiḥprāmāṇikī)sādhanārthaṃprāmāṇikamitiḥpravarttatitiyuktaṃ|napunaḥsā

pṛ0 10ba) ryatvahetuḥśarīravatvetvasmadādivadutpattipralayayogitvaṃ|taccāsyaśarīraṃsvanirmitaṃkartraṃtaranirmitaṃvāsvanirmitatveki

pṛ0 11a) tadanyathāsiddhaṃatrahipaurvāparyaparyālocanānirākṛto(paurvāparyaparyālocanayāvicāreṇanirākṛtaḥ)yathāśrutaśrutisamutpāditovākyārthaḥ|atonaivapratītimātra(pratītimātreṇaabhrāṃlavuddhayaḥ)vi

pṛ0 11ba) pākhyānānicikṣamakhamathanakāladamanakāmadāhāṃdhakavadhatrailokyākramaṇādyuparacitānivahuśaḥpaṭhaṃtaḥka

pṛ0 12a) ravātityanenoktenakrameṇeśvaranirākaraṇavacanānyevanimnamārgānusaraṇādvārīṇiteṣāṃvelā(valavṛddhiritiyāvat)samullāsastasyānuṃnnaḥ

tmanāpaṭarūpeṇanāstītisadasattvamuktaṃ|natutadyuktaṃ|kiṃkilaitāvatāpratipāditaṃsyātghaṭaḥpaṭātmanānabhavati 
ghaṭevāpaṭonāstitadetadabhimatameva|atadātmakatvenatatrāvidyamānatvātpaṭādbhidyamānoyaṃghaṭonyevetisiddhaṃsā
dhyate|tadāha|sadanyadasadanyaccatadevaṃsiddhasādhyatā|nanusvātmanāyathāghaṭaḥsvasāmarthyakriyāṃkarotyevaṃpaṭātmanā
pitatkāryaṃkuryānnacakarotyataḥpaṭātmanānāstiyadisvātmanāpiparātmavannasyāttadāsvakāryamapinakuryāttasmādasti

pṛ0 30a) canāsticetyuktaṃ|tathaitadghaṭatvamaghaṭatvaṃcaparasparamabhinnaṃ|bhedehitadvuddhyabhidhānānuvṛttirnasyātghaṭaścāghaṭaścetisāsāmā
nādhikaraṇyaṃcanabhavet|asticaitattasmādubhayātmakosaukrameṇatachavdābhidheyatāmudvahansyātghaṭaścāghaṭaścetyavirodhaḥ
etadapyuktaṃ|yatonāstikaścidabhedaḥtathāhi|aghaṭaśavdeghaṭonabhavatītiprasajyapratiṣedhovāsyātghaṭādanyaḥpaṭā
diritivāparyudāsaḥ|ādyepakṣeghaṭaścāghaṭaścasyādityukteghaṭāghaṭayoravyatirekāt|ghaṭasyābhāvevaghaṭityabhipretaṃ| 
evaṃceṣyamāṇesarpābhāvopisarpevasyāt|tathācasatisarpādivattadabhāvādapibhayaṃsyāt|sasarpaniḥsarpadeśayoḥ
samevopalaṃbhobhavet|paryudāsapakṣepighaṭatvādanyadaghaṭatvaṃbhinnamevapaṭatvādikaṃkathyate|taccaghaṭatvādbhinnaṃ|abhedehi
ghaṭādiṣvapipaṭavuddhyabhidhānānurvṛttirnasyāt|ghaṭādāvapispṛṣṭeghaṭapratyayaścanasyāt|tasmānnaghaṭāghaṭayorabhediti|na
sadasatorekāśrayattvaṃ|kiṃcaasajjaghanyaṃsachreṣṭhamityapivruvatevudhāḥ|naikatratadapekṣātaḥsthitamevobhayaṃtataḥ|yadapisa

pṛ0 30ba) dasachavdābhidheyaṃśreṣṭhāśreṣṭharūpaṃvastutadapitadvidonaikatrābhidadhati|tasyāpibhinnaviṣayatvāt|nahiyadevāśreṣṭhaṃtadeva
śreṣṭhamitivaktuṃśakyaṃ|ayamevahibhāvānāṃbhedaḥ|yadviruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ|athamataṃyattatchreṣṭāśreṣṭhatvaṃtadekatrasaṃbhavatyeva|a
pekṣāvaśāt|yathāyaṃdevadattoyajñadattasakāśādabhirūpaḥcaitrāpekṣayātunīrūpiti|evaṃtarhiyajñadattasyāśreṣṭhatvaṃ|caitrasya
caśreṣṭhatvamityubhayaṃ|tatityapekṣātaḥ|evaṃbhinnaṃlavdhaṃyadapidvayaṃtadāpekṣikatvādasatyaṃ|apekṣāhināmanavāstavī|tasyāḥki
lavastunaḥsatisadbhāvekiṃprayojanaṃ|siddhasattākatvenānapekṣatvāt|tathācāhuḥ|saṃścasarvonirāśaṃsobhāvaḥkathamape
kṣatiti|nacālavdhasattākevastunyapekṣāyāḥkimapikaraṇīyamasti|tasyāsatvādevatadapekṣānupapatteḥ| tadida
muktaṃ|apekṣānasatāṃsiddherasiddherapināsatāmiti|tadevaṃnakathaṃcidapisadasatorabhedopapattiḥ|athacettadasa
dbhāvesadayuktataroyataḥ|tatkarmasaṃkarabhayādavyāpitvaṃcatejaguḥ|sāmānyetarasaṃvaṃdhajñānābhāvādacetasaḥ|tasyadehātpūrvaṃ

pṛ0 31a) sadasatvenābhimatasyavyāpakatvātpudgalāṃtaraiḥsahakarmasaṃkaraḥśaṃkyate|vyāpakatvehiviprakīrṇānāṃkarmaṇāṃbhoktṛnpra
tiniyamaḥkiṃkṛtaḥ|avyāpakānāṃtuteṣāṃpratyekaṃvyavasthitomohādivaṃdhapadārthevatanniyāmakitikarmasaṃkarabhayādavyā
pakatvaṃacetasaḥdurvuddhayaḥjaguḥūcuḥ|kuteṣāṃmaṃdavuddhitvamityāha|sāmānyetarasaṃvaṃdhajñānābhāvāt|sāmānyenaanādi
kālīnenātmanovyāpakatvenayaḥsaṃvaṃdhaḥsarvakālabhāvitvenasādhāraṇyamasyitareṇacamuktāvevasaṃjātattvāttathārūpe
ṇataporjñānaṃsamyagavabodhasteṣāṃpudgalavādināṃnāsti|yadihitadavavodheṣāṃsyāttadānaivamayuktamevakalpayeyuḥ|mo
hādipāśāvaruddhasahajavyāpakasvabhāvasyātmanaḥsvabhāvatevāvyāpakatvābhyupagamoyastasyaviruddhatvāt|tathāhi|yaḥ
prāgavyāpakaḥsoṃtekathamanyādṛśobhavet|savikāsādidharmācettatodoṣaparaṃparā|prākpūrvaṃsaṃsārāvasthāyāmavyāpako
yaḥsakathaṃmokṣevyāpakaḥ|avyāpakatvaṃcatadānīmapyabhyupagaṃtumayuktaṃevaṃhisaṃsāramuktayoraviśeṣaḥsyāt|athatathā

pṛ0 31ba) vidhadharmasyavikāsasaṃkocadharmitvamabhyupagamyate|yadutasaṃsṛtausaṃkocametimuktautuvikasatītitatovipariṇāmitaja
ḍatvādyanekahyeṣasaṃtatiḥprasajyatitinakathaṃcidapisadasadvādimatamupasthāpayituṃśakyaṃ|idānīṃpādārthikadarśanapradarśi
tamuktinirākaraṇāyāha|ṣaṭpadārthaparijñānānmithyājñānaṃnivarttate|rāgadveṣaumamatvaṃcatadviśeṣaguṇāstataḥ|krama
śovinivarttaṃtedehasaṃyogajāyataḥ|sāmuktirjaḍatārūpātatomuktaḥśavonakiṃ|dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavā
yānāṃṣaṇmāṃpadārthānāṃsādharmyavaidharmyatatvajñānaṃniśreyasaheturityabhyupagamāt|dravyādipadārthaṣaṭkasyayathāvadjñā
nādajñānanivṛttaurāgādidoṣopaśamevuddhisukhaduḥkhechādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmādharmasaṃskārāṇāṃnavānāmātmaguṇā
nāṃdehasaṃyogatattvātkrameṇanivṛttauyāmuktiḥsāvuddhyāyuparamesatiātmanastadvinākṛtasyakiṃcidjñatvoparamājja
ḍatārūpāṃgīkriyate|tataḥśavopikiṃnamuktaḥkalpyate|tasyāpivuddhyādiguṇaviraheṇechājñānādivinākṛtatvāditi

pṛ0 32a) bhāvaḥ|athasaugatamuktinirācikīrṣayātanmatamanubhāṣyadūṣayati|cidvyaṃjakasyakarmādeḥkṣaṇikatvānmuhurmuhuḥ|vya
jyatejāyamānaivakṣaṇiketimatāparaiḥ|cidjñānaṃgrahītṛrūpaṃsaṃvedanaṃsācidvyaṃjakasyavyaktihetoḥkarmaṇovyāpārasya
yogasaṃgrahaṇāderyadivākarmaṇogrāhyasyaghaṭapaṭāderādigrahaṇātkaraṇasyacakṣurādeḥsahakāriṇālokādeścā
natisthairyātpratikṣaṇamutpadyamānaivavyajyateprakaṭībhavati|ayamarthaḥ|vakṣyamāṇasatkāryavādadṛśābhivyakti
tirobhāvabhājāmūbhāvānāṃvyāpārabhedenāvasthādeśakālādibhedenacānatisthairyāttadjñānamapi|tattadupādhibhe
dānmuhurmuhurvyajyamānaṃkṣaṇikaṃ|tadevacapramāṇamitiparemanyaṃte|tadāhuḥ|nityaṃpramāṇaṃnaivāstiprāmāṇyādva
stusaṃgateḥ|jñeyānityatayātasyādhrauvyātkramajanmaniti|cidevahipākṣaṇikātattadarthaprakāśarūpānubhava
siddhānatvetadvyatiriktātmāvidyate|saṃvedanavyatiriktasyabhedenāpratibhāṣanāt|yathāhuḥ|ekamevedaṃsavidrūpaṃvi

pṛ0 32ba) ṣaviṣādādyanekākāravivarttaṃpaśyāmaḥ|tatrayatheṣṭaṃsaṃjñāḥkriyaṃtāmiti|saṃvedanevakṣaṇikejaladhārāpravāhavatsa
dṛśaparāparotpattibhramādvikalpenasthairyamadhyāropyatityavidyājanitāseyamātmadṛṣṭiḥ|taduktaṃ|mithyāddhyārohaṇā
rthaṃcoyatno'satyapibhoktarīti|ātmagrahehisatitadanyatraparatvābhimānātsvaparabhedaḥ|tataścarāgadveṣādyanarthodbhavā
dātmagrahovaṃddhitibhagavatāsugatenanairātmyabhāvanopadiṣṭā|yaduktaṃ|ātmanisatiparasaṃjñāsvaparavibhāgātpari
grahadveṣauanayoḥsaṃprativaddhāḥsarvedoṣāḥprajāyaṃtiti|nacābhyupagatasyāpyātmanonityatvaṃghaṭateyatsattatsarvaṃkṣa
ṇikaṃ|akṣaṇikasyakramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyānupapatteḥsattāyāevāsiddhatvāt|tadāhuḥarthakriyāsamarthaṃya
ttadatraparamārthasat|asaṃtokṣaṇikāstasmātkramākramavirodhatiti|tathāhi|nityobhāvaḥkrameṇavārthakriyāṃ
kuryādyugapadvānatasyatāvatkrameṇakaraṇamupapadyate|yasmādayamapracyutonutpannasthiraikasvabhāvaḥsacakālāṃtaranirva

pṛ0 33a) rtyāmarthakriyāṃtadānīmevakiṃnakaroti|nahyasāvavasarāṃtarakaraṇīyaṃsvabhāvāṃtareṇakuryātsvabhāvasyaikarūpatvā
tsvabhāvānyathābhāvehitadvatopitadavyatirekādvināśatastataścānityatvaṃ|athocyate|sapadārthorthakriyāṃkarotye
vayadisahakāriṇosyasannihitāḥsyustadasannidhānādakurvāṇasyanāsyopālaṃbhayogyatvamiti|naivaṃ|sahakāriṇo
syākiṃcitkarāścetkimarthaṃtānapekṣate|kiṃcitkaratvetuyattatkiṃcitkurvaṃtitattato'vyatiriktaṃ|vyatiriktaṃ
vāvyatiriktakaraṇekiṃtasyatadapekṣayā|tadavyatiriktakaraṇesevasahakāribhiḥkriyatityāyātaṃ|navā
sthirasyakaraṇamupapannaṃ|tatsvabhāvasyasatasvahetubhyevotpatteḥ|kṛtasyacakarttumaśakyatvāt|athasthirasvabhā
vasyāsyasahakāriṇotiśayādhānaṃkurvaṃtiyenakālāṃtarerthakriyāṃkarotīti|etadapyasat|yasmādanāhitāti
śayātsvabhāvādāhitātiśayonyevetisthiraikasvabhāvataivatruṭyati|tathāhi|sahakāribhyotiśayotpattausapūrvo

pṛ0 33ba) tpannānāhitātiśayasvabhāvaḥsvatevavinaṣṭobhyupagaṃtavyaḥ|tasyānaśvarasvabhāvatvenanāśahetorapitatrākiṃcitka
ratvāt|vinaśvarodbhavasvabhāvatvevāvaiyarthyāttasyacasvabhāvasyāviśeṣātpratikṣaṇamitikṣaṇikatātadevaṃnanityasya
krameṇārthakriyā|nāpiyugapatkaraṇamupapadyate|yasmāyaugapadyenanirvarttitārthakriyopitasmātsvabhāvānnavira
met|sthiraikasvabhāvatvātsvabhāvaviratausvabhāvahāniḥkṣaṇikatvameva|athanāsyasvabhāvāṃtarayogaḥkiṃtuna
karotyevāyaṃkāryasyatvayaṃvipākaḥkṛtasyayatkarttumaśakyattvameva|tadapyayuktaṃ|tasyatāvadarthakriyāmakurvatausyai
vasattāyāevadurllabhatvāt|arthakriyāsamarthaṃyattadatraparamārthasadityuktatvāt|arthakriyākaraṇalakṣaṇātsvabhā

pṛ0 34a) tyātmasiddhiritipūrvaḥpakṣaḥ|siddhāṃtastutadasatkarmaṇobhogādatītānubhavasmṛteḥ|tadetatkṣaṇikatvaṃsaṃvedanasya

parakṣaṇotpattivipralavdhattvātsthairyamadhyāropitamityuktaṃ|tadayuktaṃ|svātmanikriyāvirodhādadhyāropānupapatteḥ|yada
pikṣaṇabhaṃgasādhanārthamakṣaṇikasyakramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyāvirodhātsattāyāevāsiddhatvādyatsattatkṣaṇika
mityanumāpanaghaṭanaṃkṛtaṃ|tatsvasaṃvedanalakṣaṇapramāṇavādhitatvādayuktaṃ|akṣaṇikasyasataḥsarvasyasaṃvedanasyā
nubhavasiddhatvāt|anaikāṃtikaṃcasarvasyasataḥkṣaṇikatvenavyāptipradarśanesati'kṣaṇikasyaparamārthasatovaidha
rmyadṛṣṭāṃtasyāsaṃbhavādvipakṣavyāvṛtyadarśanenahetoḥkevalānvapinogamakattvābhāvādviruddhaścāyaṃrasatvāravyohe
tuḥ|sādhyaviparyayeṇākṣaṇikatvenasarvārthakriyākāriṇighaṭādausiddhavyāptikatvātyadapyetadvyāptisādhanā

pṛ0 34ba) rthaṃnityasyakramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyāvirodhāditihetvaṃtaraṃ|tadapyāśrayāsiddhatvādayuktaṃ|nahinityaḥkaścidartho
bhavadbhirabhyupagatoyatrāyaṃhetuḥsiddhaḥsyāt|nacānityasyāpimaṇyāderarthasyakrameṇānekadeśasthāṃstāṃstānbhāvā
nprakāśayatoyugapaccaikagrahagatānghaṭapaṭādīnavabhāsayataḥkaścidvirodhaḥ|apicanatāvatkṣaṇikaṃsaṃvedanamaneka
kṣaṇānirvartyārthakriyānuṣṭhānasamarthayutpatyanaṃtaraṃdghvaṃsinastadupapattyayogāt|nacāropitaṃsthairyaṃtattadvyāpāranirvartta
nakṣamaṃ|tasyacasvātmanyadhyāropānupapatterityuktatvāt|āropyasyacāvastutvenārthakriyānuṣṭhānamasaṃvaddhaṃ|vastutve

pṛ0 35a) tamevatasyabhoktāramupalabhāmahe|dṛṣṭavaccādṛṣṭakalpanāyuktimatīyadyubhayajanmaniśarīrādibhedepyabhinnekeva

pṛ0 35ba) nahiprāktanakṣaṇānubhūtasukhaduḥkhādisaṃvedanaṃvarttamānenakālāṃtarabhāvināvākṣaṇenasmarttuṃśakyaṃ|tasyānyatvāt
|yathānadevadattānubhūtaṃtatputraḥsmarati|dṛśyatecaśaiśavādyanubhūtaṃyauvanasthāvirāyavasthāsusmaryamāṇaṃ|sāveyamatītā

bhūtyaikapramāṇatvāt|tadevaṃkarmopabhogādatītānubhavasmṛteścanakṣaṇikatvamupapadyatecitaḥ|tadāha|sthitirniranva

pṛ0 36a) tinamuktāvapyupaplavaḥ|nacāstyanubhavaḥkaścidbhavāvasthāvaraṃtataḥ|śuddhacitsaṃtatisamutpādobhavatāṃmokṣaḥsyāt|yadivā
pradīpanirvāṇarūpaḥ|ādyepakṣesvaparikalpitasyapratikṣaṇavināśitvalakṣaṇasyopaplavasyamuktāvapyanupaśamaḥ|dvi
tīyetupradīpanirvāṇarūpattvātsaṃvedanāsaṃbhavataḥ kaścidapyanubhavonāstītitathāvidhātsaṃvedanavināśātmakā

yatāvedāṃtasāṃkhyasadasatpadārthikādimateṣvasarvadarśipraṇetṛtvādvastusaṃgrahasyāsphuṭatvamasaṃvaddhatāṃcodbhāvyada

pṛ0 36ba) kuśalamatayoyemuktimichaṃtitekhadyotādagnyabhyarthinaḥkīṭamaṇervahniṃlipsavasteyathāvyarthaśramāstathātevipha
lakleśābhavaṃtītyarthaḥ|tathāhi|yattkaivalyaṃpuṃprakṛtyorvivekādyovāsarvaṃvrahmamatvāvirāmaḥ|yāvākāścinmuktayaḥpāśa

stutesamabhavanprakṛteḥparasyāḥkṛṣṇāniruddhamakaradhvajarauhiṇeyāiti|yathāvākālaikavādināṃ|kālaḥsṛja

pṛ0 37a) dhānaśavdenatatvamuktamacetanaṃ|tasmātpara(nāstyaparaṃyeṣāṃbhāvaḥpratiṣṭhitaḥ|natemuktāmunivyāghrapunarāyāṃtyadhogatimiti|tathāupādāneṣulīnānāṃpunarāvarttanaṃdhruvamiti||teṣāṃtuyathāvidhasiddhādhyāsabhājāṃnaniraṃ)taramajñānadhvāṃtasaṃtatiraṃtaḥkaraṇamāvṛṇoti|apitutekrameṇavivekapra

pṛ0 37ba) nāṃmalakarmamāyāparameśvaranirodhaśaktīnāmuparatādhikāratvātpunaḥsargaprāraṃbhenādhobhyeti|nasaṃsārībhavatī

pṛ0 57ba) tvāt|athaparārthāścakṣurādayaḥ|saṃghātatvāt|śayanādyaṃgavatityādinākaraṇādīnāṃkarttṛprayojyatvādināvānumā

pṛ0 58a) dauśayaṃ|yauvanasthāvirabhojanalaṃghanādihetukaucadehasaṃvaṃdhināvupacayāpacayāvanukurvadvijñānaṃdehātmakameva|atodeha

pṛ0 58ba) dvā|yasminsatiyasyabhāvopadabhāvecābhāvastattatkāryaṃ|śītamivahimasya|saticaśuklaśoṇitātmakadehāraṃbhakabhūtaviśe

pṛ0 59a) bhakabhūtasadbhāvepinacaitanyasaṃbhavogarbhāvasthāyāṃcasakalasāmagrīsadbhāvepikadāciccaitanyāsaṃbhavastasmātsatisatvamanaikāṃti

pṛ0 59ba) tiḥkiṃnaḥchinnamiticettadapyayuktamityāha|nāpyevaṃsupratītatvātsmarttākāyetarostyataḥ|sakalalokaprasiddhasmṛtyapahna

pṛ0 60a) dyanvayagrahaṇamanumānāṃgaṃkalpanīyaṃ|dharmiṇīcadehepakṣīkṛtetadgatasyakāṭhinyādeḥpṛthivyādidharmatvaniścayātpṛthivyādi

pṛ0 61a) ti|nacatachivatvamavyāpakatvādidharmayuktaṃ|jñatvakarttṛtvarahitaṃvā|nityaṃkālānavachedādvaitatpātrapradeśagamityādināpratyutatadvi

pṛ0 61ba) kṣepataḥkathyate|yeṣāmapagamepaśuṃttvānmuktāṇavaḥātmānojagataḥpatayobhavaṃti|tatraśivavadanyānadhīnasvātaṃtryābhi

pṛ0 62ba) sāmarthyasyānviṣpatyapekṣate|muktinimittaṃcakathaṃśāṃbhavaṃvalamanveṣṭi|nānyathāvalaṃpratīkṣate|pāśānabhyupagamesatisvabhā

pṛ0 63ba) theti|yaditutadaṃjanamatonyathetihetumanapekṣyaidātmānamāśliṣyati|tadānīṃmuktānapyātmanoruṇaddhi|uparuddhadṛkkriyā

pṛ0 64a) thaikatvesatyekasmātpuruṣādapasṛtetasminsarveṣāṃtulyakālaṃmuktiḥsyāt|yatastunamuktiryogapadyenopalabhyatetasmātkāra

pṛ0 71ba) kāryakāraṇapratītiniyamāsaṃbhavesatyatatsvabhāvādapitatsvabhāvasyotpattiprāpteḥ|yathānadṛṣṭādhūmālliṃgātkadācidapi

pṛ0 72a) raṇādyutpadyamānamupalabhyate|tataḥkimadṛṣṭenamāyādinā|kāraṇenakḷptenetyāśaṃkyāha|śarīrādeḥśarīrādiyaditanni

pṛ0 77ba) ratvaṃ|padutatenaprakāśarūpeṇajñānaśaktiprarocinā|sarvakārakaniṣpādyamavaitiviṣayaṃparaṃ|tenavidyākhyenatatvenaprakāśakatvā

tobhogyeti|tathā|ravivatprakāśarūpoyadināmamahāṃstathāpikarmatvāt|karaṇāṃtarasāpekṣaḥśaktogrāhayitumātmā

|tenavidyākhyenatatvenābhivyaktajñānaśaktitvāddṛṣṭārthopyaṇurapipāsito'saṃjātābhilāṣaḥsannaitinabhogyāharaṇāyaga

pṛ0 78a) pispṛhan|ādattenacabhuṃjānovirāgamadhigachati|tenarāgeṇaraṃjitojanitābhiṣvaṃgaḥsoṇurmalinatamamapimāyīyabhogyamabhi

pṛ0 78ba) tthānānnipatyāniyataṃpaśuṃ|truṭilavanimeṣamuhūrttādeḥpratyayasyajñānasyārthonimittaṃyaḥsakālaḥsamuhūrttamāstepraharaṃbhrāmya

pṛ0 79a) yaccayāvaccayatracaśubhāśubhamātmakarma|tasmāccatenacatathācatadācataccatāvaccatatracavidhātvaśādupaiti|athātraparihāraḥ|bho

pṛ0 79ba) tipuṃpratīterhetuḥ|anenamāyāgarbhasthebhyodhikārirudrāṇubhyastattadbhuvananivāsibhyaścavailakṣaṇyamuktaṃ|teṣāṃpuṃpratyayā

pṛ0 80a) dahaṃkārayostanmātrapaṃcakasyetyevaṃsaptānāṃgraṃthīnāṃkāryayonīnāmudbhavahetorgauṇasyatatvasyakāraṇaṃ|yathāhitasyakāṃcanaratnā

pṛ0 80ba) guṇānāmidaṃsātvikamidaṃrājasamidaṃtāmasamityādikāekaikaśrutirvṛtyādhikyahetukī|adhikavṛttisatvaṃyasyasasā

pṛ0 81a) vuddhiguṇādharmajñānavairāgyabhūtayaḥ|sātvikāvyatyayenaiterāgamutsṛjyatāmasāḥ|dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryākhyāvuddhiguṇābhāvasaṃ

pṛ0 81ba) dakauṣṭhyakauṇyāniklaivyodāvarttayaṃgutāiti|vuddhivadhāścaprāguktānāṃtuṣṭisiddhīnāṃviparyayāḥsaptadaśetyaṣṭāviṃśatiḥ|

pṛ0 82a) bhātivainayikoguṇaḥ|yastusadyathāvaddhitalokabuddhergurutaḥśāstrādvāsamarjitaḥsavainayikobhātiprakāśate|samarjito

pṛ0 82ba) rdehavuddhirvighnaścārthāsteṣusāṃsiddhikeṣu|dharmādiṣusāṃsiddhikeṣusatsvidamidaṃsaṃpadyatitipūrveṇasaṃvaṃdhaḥ|kiṃkimityatrāha

pṛ0 83a) cetyetāvatāsaṃkṣiptaprabhedakathanenavuddhiviplavoyaḥśaṃkyatesaḥ|evaṃvidhātuṣṭiḥitthaṃvidhāsiddhirityevaṃsāmānyalakṣaṇesaṃkṣi

pṛ0 83ba) miśrāguṇājanakāityuktaṃyataḥ|tuṣṭirmithyāsvarūpatvāttamoguṇanibaṃdhanā|sukharūpatayāvrahmansātvikyapyavasīyate|

myate|aśaktirapravṛttatvāttāmasīduḥkhabhāvataḥ|rājasyapiguṇodṛṣṭaḥkāryekāraṇasaṃśrayaḥ|apravṛttirūpāśaktiru
ktā|ataścāsmaitāmasīduḥkhahetutvāccarājasyapivijñeyā|yohikārye'śaktyākhyeduḥkharūpoprabhaviṣṇutārūpovāguṇosti

pṛ0 84a) athātramuniḥsāṃkhyachāyayāpraśnayati|vuddhirvodhanimittaṃcedvidyātadvyatiricyate|rāgopisatyavairāgyekalāyoniḥkaroti
kiṃ|uktayānītyāvodhanimittatvaṃvuddheryaducyatetadvidyāyāānarthakyaṃ|tasyāpivodhahetutvenābhyupagamāṃt|yadicasāmānya
vyāpāratvepitadabhyupagamaḥtadānavasthā|kiṃcaavairāgyalakṣaṇevuddhidharmerāgarūpesatyapikalājanmārāgaḥkiṃkaroti

etaccabhogasthānaṃsaṃsāramaṃḍalaṃparamārthavidaḥkathayaṃti|prākproktaṃsakalaṃbhogyaṃbhuvanajātaṃkalādibhyojyotibhyojāyate
|natumāyātaḥ|tasyāḥśrīmatsvāyaṃbhuvādāvakāryatvaśruteḥ|(akāryatvaśruteḥ)sanniveśavatītvebhogyatvābhyupagamecabhogyasyasaṃniveśavato
ghaṭāderivānityatvaprasaṃgabhayādasanniveśavatītvamabhogyatvaṃcāvaśyamaṃgīkāryaṃ|nanupuṃsomāyāyadyabhogyātarhitatkārya

pṛ0 97ba) vyatirekeṇānyasyabhogyasyāsaṃbhavānnakiṃcidbhogyamityabhoktaivapuruṣaḥprasaktaḥ|satyaṃ|kiṃtukāryaṃtadīyaṃvyaktarūpatayā'vasthi
taṃ|tadasyabhogyaṃnatvasauśaktimātrarūpatvāttasyā(māyāvījarūpatvāt)ityuktaṃ|yadyevaṃtadgarbhetattadbhuvanaviśeṣaśruterbhuvanānāṃcasanniveśaviśiṣṭatāṃ
vinābhuvanatvāyogānmāyātatvasyāpisanniveśavatvaṃprasaktamityāha|māyāyāmapipaṭhyaṃtegahaneśādayodhipāḥ|tatvaśuddhi
ścadīkṣāyāṃsarvaṃtatkṛtimastake|nityatvavyāpakatvādiśravaṇādavasīyate|draṣṭaṃpurādiyadbhogyaṃmūrttaṃpralayadharmaca|yadyapi
māyātatvemaṃḍaleśvaragahaneśvarādayo(maṃḍaleśābhinnāḥgahaneśvarāścabhinnāḥ)rudrābhuvanādhivāsinaḥpaṭhyaṃte|yadvakṣyati|dhruvatejodhipaurudraugahaneśaścasarvarāṭ| 
māyādhikāriṇorudrāmaṃḍalādhipatīśvarāiti|dīkṣāyāmapitatvaśuddhirmāyātatvasthatvenayadyapikriyāpādebhidhā

pṛ0 98a) nmūrddhanyetatsarvaṃsthitamityarthaḥ|kutetadjñāyatityāha|nityatvavyāpakatvādityādiyatonityatvaṃvyāpakatvaṃcāsyāprāgupapādi

pṛ0 98ba) yojanapramāṇaṃparamāṇvādikrameṇavarṇayati|rajovilokyatetiryakjālāviṣṭārkarociṣāṃ|tadaṣṭāṣṭaguṇasthānetṛtīyesyā

pṛ0 99a) dhanurdvayenadaṃḍodaṃḍasahasradvayenakrośaḥ|krośadvayenagavyūtiḥ|tatdvayaṃcayojanaṃ|eṣāṃyojanānāmarvudaṃkoṭivistṛtaṃvrahmāṃ

pṛ0 99ba) lakṣāṇitadaṃtarāṇidvātriṃśat|tebhyekaikaṃlakṣapramāṇaṃ|etānipāpīyasāṃpātanārthaṃkṛtānyadhvavedhasābhuvanādhvasraṣṭā'naṃte

pṛ0 100a) ṭayastriṃśatdvecalakṣedvijottama|triṃśadyojanakoṭyolakṣadvayaṃcetitrayastriṃśatpuṭānāṃsāṃtarālānāṃpramāṇaṃ|tatastriṃśatsahasrā

pṛ0 100ba) hasrikī|tadāsāṃsaptakaṃsadbhiḥkhyātaṃpātālasaptakaṃ|kāṃcanamāyasaṃcayatpuṭadvayamuktaṃtatonaṃtaramiyaṃvasuṃdharābhūmiraṣṭamī

pṛ0 102ba) ttarāḥ|ekādaśasahasrātumanusāhasrikīparā|nemiryāmastakopāṃtelokapālasamāśrayā|cakravāṭetitāmāhusarvaratnaprabhā

pṛ0 103a) nnivodhata|spaṣṭaḥślokaḥ|nānāratnaprabhātālamaṃḍalālaṃkṛtāhareḥ|siddhasādhyamarujjuṣṭārukmabhūramarāvatī|vividharannakāṃtika

pṛ0 103ba) vicitraratnaviracitāsamarthabhūtaniṣevitāvāyoḥpurīgaṃdhavatīnāma|mahodayācaṃdramasaḥśvetāmuktādinirmitā|dvijasaṃ

pṛ0 104a) joruṇatvenamanoramatvamāsāṃyuktaṃ|athavrahmaṇaḥpurīcaturdaśasahasrāṇiyojanānāṃsvayaṃbhuva||madhyemanovatīnāmapurīloke

pṛ0 104ba) |mūrddhnidevādidevasyasthānaṃtripuravidviṣaḥ|rudrāyutagaṇairjuṣṭaṃvrahmādyaiścasurottamaiḥ|tasyajyotiṣkasyasamabhūbhāgeṣuprakṛṣṭānāṃsa

pṛ0 105a) nivarṣāṇiyeṣāṃ|tadarddhenāntaviṣkaṃbhaumālyavadgaṃdhamādanau|yāmyottarauprākpratīcyormerutastāvadaṃtarau|niṣadhādiśailamānārddhenagṛhī

pṛ0 105ba) kṣārddhonnatapaḥkḷptāḥsteṣāṃpūrveṇamaṃdaraḥ|śvetoharidrācūrṇābhoyāmyatogaṃdhamādanaḥ|pratīcyāṃvipulonīlaḥsupārśvaḥsaumyatoru

pṛ0 106a) danaṃmānasaṃtatrasaromānasataskaraṃ|dakṣiṇeviṣkaṃbhaśailamūlenaṃdanākhyamupavanaṃsaraścātramanohārimānasaṃnāma|vaibhrājaṃvai

pṛ0 107a) srāṇidvādaśāyurhiraṇvati|janasyeṃdutviṣonityamaśnatolākucaṃphalaṃ|hiraṇyavatsaṃjñevarṣelakucaphalāśinaścaṃdrakāṃterjanasya

pṛ0 107ba) satathāvidhaḥ|etānivuddhyādīnīkṛtādiṣuyugeṣujanasyayathāyathaṃhrāsavaṃtītyarthaḥ|ādhyātmikādhidaivikādhibhautika

pṛ0 108a) ratākhyeprakīrttitāḥ|etenavadvīpāvividhajātijanapadasaṃkulāḥbhāratanāmnivarṣekathitāḥ|dvīpaśabdorddharcāddipāṭātpuṃsya

pṛ0 108ba) khyovṛkṣunnataḥśākākhyāhetuḥ|vrahmaṇiyaṣṭumichatihemakuśotpattiḥkauśedvīpekuśasaṃjñākāraṇaṃ|krauṃce**?kācanakaṃ

pṛ0 109a) yathākiṃpuruṣādyeṣukṛtāvāsaḥsadājanaḥ|śākadvīpādiṣutathākṣīrādikṛtabhojanaḥ|himeṃduhimanīlābjasasyakaspha

thāśākādidvīpeṣukṣīrādyāhāroyathākramaṃtuhinacaṃdrādikāṃtirdaśavarṣasahasrajīvīduḥkhākhyamahākaṃṭakanirmuktojñe
yaḥ|saptamādudadhervāgdvekoṭīsatrikaṃdalaṃ|paṃcāśaccasahasrāṇikarṇāddhemādrigarbhataḥ|saptamādudadherarvāgitimervā

tkoṭidvayaṃ|trikenasahitaṃdalaṃkoṭyarddhaṃtripaṃcāśallakṣāṇisahasrapaṃcāśatpramāṇaṃ|tatohiraṇmayībhūmirnānāratnadrumācalā

pṛ0 109ba) śṛṃgaparichadaḥ|aṃtaḥsthitaṃlokaṃvahisthitaṃcālokaṃniyamayatītilokālokaniyāmakaḥ|tadvāhyelokābhāvityarthaḥ|yojanā

ṣṭyāddhāmānyāśābhṛtāṃmune|lokālokasyādreratyunnatatvāchikharasthānilokapālaviharaṇagṛhāṇyarkabhābhirviprakṛṣṭatvā

pṛ0 110a) ṭisvarllokasvargivaryasamāśrayaḥ|mahadvikoṭiryatrāstemarīcyādimunivrajaḥ|janoṣṭakoṭyavachinnaḥpitṛjahnujanāśrayaḥ|taporka

pṛ0 110ba) vrahmāṃḍagolakaḥ|bhūyasātulyevāyaṃsarvasrotaḥsamānataḥ|taduparicatasraḥkoṭayomadhuvidviṣaḥviṣṇoḥkāṃtimatsthānaṃ|tripura

pṛ0 111a) śvaryairyuktāpyetesvaṃsvaṃsthānaṃpālakatvenanojhaṃti|etecānekākārairvicitrāyudhairativalairvālārkakāṃtibhiranucarairvṛtāḥ|athaiṣāṃ

pṛ0 111ba) napūrvamuktayoretepatayaḥ|vidyeśasarvavidjñānavedavighneṣṭhavedayāḥ|vidyāvidhātṛbhūteśavalipriyasukhādhipāḥ|īśānadiksthāne

pṛ0 112a) śaucetiguhyāṣṭakamidaṃjale|etadaṃbhastatvasthaṃguhyāṣṭakaṃ|śrīśailajalpakedārabhairavāmrātakeśvarāḥ|hariścaṃdramahākālamadhyāḥsāti

pṛ0 112ba) vrāhmaṃdīptaṃparamayāśriyā|vuddhitatvesūkṣmabhuvanāṣṭakaṃteṣupaiśācamāditityādaupaiśācaṃ|tatorākṣasādi|brāhmaṃdīptaṃparamayāśriye

pṛ0 113a) krodheśacaṃḍasaṃvarttajyotiḥpiṃgalasūragāḥ|paṃcāṃtakaikavīraucaśikhedititesmṛtāḥ|vāma devādibhyaḥprāguktebhyodaśaguṇāśrī

pṛ0 113ba) tejodhipaurudraugahaneśaścaviśvarāṭ|māyādhikāriṇorudrāmaṃḍalādhipatīśvarāḥsaṃsāracakrakārūṭabhūtagrāmavivarttakāḥ|

pṛ0 114a) praśnānuṣaṃgataḥ|bhṛguṇīprabhṛtīnāṃvidyārājñīnāmūrdhvaṃvidyādhipākhyorudrodīptaśrīrniratiśayajñānakriyāsaṃpadyuktaḥsthitaḥ|

pṛ0 114ba) parichadāḥparivāroyasyasevaṃvidhaḥsadāśivanāthaḥsthitaḥ|vaiṃdavetusthānenivṛttipratiṣṭhāvidyāśāṃtyākhyakalādhipati

pṛ0 115a) sthitiḥ|sarvātiśayaviśrāmastadūrdhvaṃpatayaḥkathaṃ|sadāśivatatvādūrdhvaṃśivatatvāṃtaṃyāvadyo'dhvātatrāṇūnāṃnivṛtyādikaleśva
rāṇāṃdhvanipatiprabhṛtīnāṃcavapusthitirdivyadehayogaḥkalpitaḥ|tatracasadāśivatatvesarvātiśayaviśrāmaḥ|nahitatonyanni
ṣkalaśivatatvādṛteatiśāyikimapyastievaṃcakathaṃtadūrdhvaṃpatayuktāḥ|siddhāṃtaḥ|īśānatītyaśāṃtāṃtaṃtatvaṃcasādāśivaṃsmṛ
taṃ|bhuvanānyapinādādikālānānyaḥpatiḥśivāt|anaṃteśādividyeśvaropalakṣitamīśvaratattvamatikramyaniṣkalatatvāṃ

kaścit|tataścatadūrdhvaṃpatayaḥkathamityasyacodyasyānavakāśaḥ|yadyevaṃnādedhvanipatiḥśaktausarvaśaktimatāṃvaritibhi
nnatvaṃ|patīnāṃyaduktaṃtatkathamityāha|kiṃtuyaḥpatibhedosminsaśāstreśaktibhedavat|kṛtyabhedopacāreṇatadbhedasthānabhedataḥ 
|śivabhaṭṭārakādanyonātrādhipatiḥ|kiṃtunivṛtyādikaleśvarāṇāṃyosmiṃchāstrebhedaḥsayathaikasyāevaśakteḥkṛtyabhedopacā

pṛ0 115ba) (reṇatadbhedasthānabhedataḥ|śivabhaṭṭārakādanyonātrādhipatiḥ|kiṃtunivṛtyādikaleśvarāṇāṃyosmiṃchāstrebhedaḥsayathaikasyāeva
śakteḥkṛtyabhedopacā)reṇavāmādibhedabhinnatvamevaṃsthānabhedahetukaḥkṛtyabhedakṛtaścajñeyaḥ|tamevakāryabhedaṃdarśayituṃviṃdubhuvanastha
nivṛtyādikaleśvaracatuṣṭayasyasādhāraṇaṃtāvadrūpamāha|karotyunmīlanaṃyābhiḥśaktibhirnaratejasaḥ|tānivṛtyādisaṃjñānāṃbhuva
nānāmadhīśvarāḥ|āṇavasyasarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvātmanastejasopābhiḥśaktibhiḥ prakāśanaṃdevaḥkurutetāḥśaktayevanivṛtti
patiṣṭhādisaṃjñānāṃbhuvanānāmīśvaratvenoktāḥ|ityetatsamānamāsāṃsvarūpaṃ|athāsādhāraṇaṃcāhanivarttayatibhūtāni

tisaṃsāravainṛṣṇyajananenakaivalyābhimukhaṃsaṃpādayati|sābhagavataḥśaktirbhavanivarttanavyāpārakāriṇītaccatadadhiṣṭheyaṃsthā

pṛ0 116a) pisaṃjñeyaṃ|tathāhi|nivṛttasyagatirbhūyoyayāpracyāvalakṣaṇā|niṣidhyatepratiṣṭhāsāsthānaṃtadvāṃścatatpatiḥ|abhidhyānānu

vidyāsthānamapyasyāvidyeśaścatadīśvaraḥ|āptaviṣayaṃśavdaviṣayaṃgamyaviṣayaṃcānumeyaṃjñānaṃtyaktvāpāriśepyātsākṣātkṛ

pṛ0 116ba) citoyābhirvimucyataḥ|tāiṃdhikādyāstatsthānaṃtadvānīśastisṛṣvapi|vimocanaṃvimuktirvadhāpagamaḥ|vimucamichatyātmana

pṛ0 117a) rdhvagākhyaṃ|tadadhiṣṭhātāceśvarūrdhvagādhipatirityuktaḥ|yepitatpadamāpannāḥśaivasādhanayogataḥ|tetatsthityaṃtamāhlādaṃprāpya

pṛ0 117ba) ityevamuktapārītyāeṣasadāśivanāthaḥkṛtyayogātsthityādivyāpāravaśātsakalaḥsahikalābhivrahmapaṃcakasaṃvaṃdhinīmi

pṛ0 118a) tyapirajastamobhyāmanyaṃtvenaviṣayatvāsaṃbhavātpāśatvasiddheḥ|tathācoktaṃsiddhaguruṇāsatvaguṇāvachannobhavināṃbhogomalo

pṛ0 118ba) dbhuvanaṃnirvarttyatattatkṛtyopayoginaḥsaṃbhavaṃti|yathoktamāgamāṃtare|pārthivaprākṛtāṃḍābhyāmūrdhvamaṃḍadvayepare|śuddhāḥsatvādayaḥ

pṛ0 119a) ginoḥ|vilayaḥprātilomyenaśaktitatvadvayāvadhiḥ|evamityuktenaprakāreṇakramikoyaḥprasavaḥsargastadyoginordvayorapiśu

pṛ0 119ba) vilayaḥ|svechāparigṛhītasadāśivādisakalarūpopasaṃhṛtalakṣaṇemahāsvāpesamastasyāpyadhvanaḥpratisaṃcaraḥ|yathāyādṛśa

pṛ0 120a) dhikṛtairupasaṃhṛtyavyāpāraḥkriyate|athakathaṃtadadhikṛtāstadānīmāsatityāha|tadārudraśataṃvīraśrīkaṃṭhaucapradhānayāḥ|

pṛ0 120ba) ddhiguṇādharmajñānavairāgyabhūtayityādinā|nahiguṇinaḥsakāśādguṇasyāpagamodṛṣṭaḥ|nahipaṭāchauklyaṃśarkarādermādhuryaṃ

pṛ0 121a) dyanukṛtaucetilakṣaṇāṃsūrayojaguḥ|mukhyārthānugamānupapatterguṇārthaṃpratipratītikāriṇīṃlakṣaṇāṃsūrayastadvirojaguḥūcuḥ

pṛ0 121ba) cāraḥkṛtaḥsamīpārthalakṣaṇāyathāgaṃgāyāṃghoṣiti|atrahimukhyayāvṛttyāpravahajjalarūpaṃsrotaḥguṇavṛttyāvātadviśeṣaṃyadi

pṛ0 133ba) tyatratutṛtīyasmiṃścacatuṣkebhujalayorvādyagnyoścānyonyaṃpoṣakatvānmitratvamityevaṃparijñāyavudhvāprājñoyogītathātathāsādha
